## Callees

- 0x78EC20 CChitin::SuspendThreads
- 0x7E8317 sprintf
- 0x7E9BB9 FUN_007e9bb9
- 0x7E9BD0 _strrchr

## Notes

Ours ports the original line for line except the file-name split. The
original does `strrchr(szFileName, '\\') + 1`: MSVC spells `__FILE__` with
backslashes, so there is always one. GCC and Clang spell it with `/`, so on
Linux `strrchr` returns NULL and `NULL + 1` is passed to `sprintf` and
`CChitin::ShutDown`, which faults and leaves `log.txt` empty. Ours takes the
name after the last `\\` or `/`, and the whole string when there is neither.
For a path with backslashes only (every MSVC build) the result is the
original's.

Found by the Linux scripted play-through of
`scenarios/pending/world_entry.txt`: the `LoadGame` assertion at
`src/CInfGame.cpp:2689` ended in a silent SIGSEGV inside `sprintf`. With the
fix the assertion text reaches the log (see the task-7 report).

No oracle case: the function suspends the threads, shuts the engine down and
aborts, so it cannot run in the oracle runner.
